function dangerMove(p,q){if(retreat)return null;for(const e of enemies()){const r=['skeleton','stray','witch','pillager'].includes(e.name)?17:(e.name==='creeper'?((e.metadata?.[16]===1)?7:3.6):5);if(Math.min(e.position.distanceTo(p),e.position.distanceTo(q))>r)continue;if(![p,q].some(t=>rayClear(e.position.offset(0,(e.height||1.8)*.8,0),t.offset(0,1.1,0))))continue;if(!retreat||e.position.distanceTo(q)<e.position.distanceTo(p)-.08)return e}return null}
async function defendClose(){const p=bot.entity.position,a=p.offset(0,1.62,0);const cs=enemies().map(e=>{const w=(e.width||.6)/2,clamp=(n,l,h)=>Math.max(l,Math.min(h,n));const aim=new Vec3(clamp(a.x,e.position.x-w,e.position.x+w),clamp(a.y,e.position.y+.1,e.position.y+(e.height||1.8)-.1),clamp(a.z,e.position.z-w,e.position.z+w));return{e,aim}}).filter(o=>o.aim.distanceTo(a)<2.9&&rayClear(a,o.aim)).sort((a,b)=>a.aim.distanceTo(p)-b.aim.distanceTo(p));if(!cs.length||bot.health<8)return false;const ax=bot.inventory.items().find(i=>/sword$/.test(i.name))||bot.inventory.items().find(i=>i.name==='stone_axe'&&i.durabilityUsed<124);if(!ax)return false;stop();await bot.equip(ax,'hand');await bot.lookAt(cs[0].aim,true);const before=cs[0].e.metadata?.[9];bot.attack(cs[0].e);await tick(25);console.log('ACTUAL_DEFEND',cs[0].e.id,before,bot.entities[cs[0].e.id]?.metadata?.[9],bot.health);return true}
async function manual(q){const start=bot.entity.position.clone();if(!full(bot.blockAt(q.offset(0,-1,0)))||![0,1].every(y=>clear(bot.blockAt(q.offset(0,y,0)))))throw Error('STEP_GEOMETRY_'+q);if(q.y<Math.floor(start.y)&&!clear(bot.blockAt(q.offset(0,2,0))))throw Error('DESCENT_HEAD');for(let k=0,stable=0;k<85;k++){check();const p=bot.entity.position,d=Math.hypot(p.x-q.x-.5,p.z-q.z-.5);stop();if(bot.entity.onGround&&Math.abs(p.y-q.y)<.12&&d<.12){if(++stable>=1)return}else stable=0;const foe=dangerMove(p,q.offset(.5,0,.5));if(foe){console.log('CURRENT_STEP_THREAT',foe.id,foe.name,foe.position,p,q,retreat);if(retreat&&await defendClose())continue;throw Error('STEP_THREAT_'+foe.id)}if(d>.045){await bot.lookAt(new Vec3(q.x+.5,p.y+1.4,q.z+.5),true);bot.setControlState('forward',true);bot.setControlState('sneak',!retreat&&q.y>=Math.floor(start.y))}bot.setControlState('jump',q.y>p.y+.1);await tick()}throw Error('STEP_NOT_ARRIVED_'+q)}
function movements(){const m=new Movements(bot);m.canDig=false;m.canOpenDoors=false;m.allowSprinting=false;m.allowParkour=false;m.allow1by1towers=false;m.scafoldingBlocks=[];m.maxDropDown=1;for(const n of['water','lava','fire','magma_block','sweet_berry_bush','nether_portal','powder_snow'])if(bot.registry.blocksByName[n])m.blocksToAvoid.add(bot.registry.blocksByName[n].id);const prior=m.getNeighbors.bind(m);m.getNeighbors=n=>prior(n).filter(q=>{const p=new Vec3(q.x+.5,q.y,q.z+.5);return !threat(p)&&Math.abs(q.y-n.y)<=1});bot.pathfinder.setMovements(m);bot.pathfinder.thinkTimeout=2000;}
async function go(q,ms=12000){if(threat(q.offset(.5,0,.5)))throw Error('TARGET_THREAT');movements();let timer;const oldHits=hurts.length;try{await Promise.race([bot.pathfinder.goto(new goals.GoalBlock(q.x,q.y,q.z)),new Promise((_,r)=>timer=setTimeout(()=>{stop();r(Error('NAV_BUDGET'))},ms))])}finally{clearTimeout(timer);stop()}await tick(3);if(hurts.length!==oldHits||!bot.entity.onGround||!bot.entity.position.floored().equals(q))throw Error('NAV_POSTCONDITION');}
